# Chapter Summaries

Rolling window for voice and continuity. Batches 0001–0005 complete (ch 1–58). **Volume 1 complete.**

## Volume 1 — A Voice in the Dark (ch 1–58)

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 1 | The Bell at Ninth Watch | Ning Yan, four hands short before dawn bell, hears Yuan (name/stage/breaths) in staging ground. Tests him with Ning Wu thought, speaks name aloud; Shen Xiu hears sound not his own. Chooses run over infirmary. Stage 3. |
| 2 | Ashjaw | First Verge night, watchtower line. Yuan offers quest (clear tower, take plate, cost half-stage). She accepts, kills listed Ashjaw with Nine-Step alone. Merit settles warm into Yuan, Settlement drops sliver. Lu Fen eyes claim. First pay. |
| 3 | What It Took | Two seniors contest plate; Xie Qiao killing stroke stopped when Yuan spends Merit without consent. She wakes stage 9→4, scar sternum-hip, Nine-Step gone from head. Asks what it took; he does not answer. Irreversible. |
| 4 | Nine Steps | Third day, lame leg, head empty. Footwork returns as habit not technique. Chooses to walk not rest to avoid Shen Xiu count. Stops blaming, starts watching. Wants his number; he withholds. |
| 5 | The Second Watch | Holds tower in wind. Gives Ashjaw claim to Du Hong (18, ambitious, bad at lying), keeps pine record plate. Wins argument without fight via shoulder-reading. Makes first friend unknowingly; now answerable to him. |
| 6 | What the Layer Says | Night announcement heard bound-wide: Mei Jiu to stage 8. Learns layer is room not sky, announces to all equally, not on her side. Counts seconds, deduces everywhere-at-once. Yuan small inside it. |
| 7 | The Thirty-Third | Dawn announcement: harvest 33, servant Lin Shou taken; yard believed 28. Layer reports without editorializing. Cold anger; vows to find what 33rd was for. Shen Xiu ashen. Needs record/kiln. |
| 8 | The Tree Line | Inner tree line, nine-sheath. Second quest stated (full stage + aperture risk). Refuses Du Hong inside radius. Solo clear, 4→5 mid-fight, rim holds. Layer announces unfiled kill/bounty. Visible. |
| 9 | Deadweight | Fifth day carries broken-leg Pei Lan (19, furnace-crew) with Du Hong; Shen Xiu silently takes half-weight two miles. Gives only coat; fever breaks. First unselfish arithmetic, cold/exhaustion/reputation. Route past Lowwater noted. |
| 10 | A Fair Hand | Lowwater Lantern tower sixth evening. Feng Lushi (consumptive, 22 yrs fair hand) believes her, writes voice down, copies plate. 19 Listeners, job eats them. Copy date post-dates brother's kiln closure. Unexpected ally. |
| 11 | The Return | Seventh day road to Lower Gate with eight bodies. Shen Xiu leaves fourth loss line open; she carries Pei Lan in on own feet before yard so line cannot be written. 5→6 via load. Announces self unintentionally. Ledger reading next. |
| 12 | The Reading of the Ledger | Eighth morning yard reading: Ning Bo six-year unsettled account carried to nearest unspent thing — Ning Yan held as credit in furnace column (three-generations rule). Token settled whole via proxy. Shifts want from death-paper to truth-paper; seeks furnace column meaning. |
| 13 | Public Ground | Day after reading, outer yard/terrace. Learns furnace column is account settled in kind/service — family billed six years in air. Du Hong urges leave-alone, protects from number he does not know; she asks if sect would settle honestly, takes his no seriously. Anger cools to useful. Wants books under furnace. |
| 14 | The Book of Accounts | Training yard, losing badly. Winter drill in nine days. Body 6 remembers 9; performs step three as habit. Asks Yuan if he took something else; true+incomplete answer, both let lie stand — first deliberate omission. Breaks 6→7 via work. Wants sponsor for past-9. |
| 15 | The Map She Was Not Given | Scripture hall. Map not locked, simply absent for names not on list. Mei Jiu reveals past-9 is document problem needing sect seal, servant-born cannot be sealed; tells true cost (mother's labour, sponsor fee, clerk wait). Rival → person with own account. Pull: Lowwater non-sect records. |
| 16 | Wen the Lamp-mender | Lowwater road daylight. Lantern will not stop, Wen will not answer — then one answer: no, not sky, somebody speaks it; weather vs office, layer is office, Bureau visible part. Ning chooses not to push — first chosen not-knowing. Discovery: administered sky. Pull: Feng has brother's name. |
| 17 | Fair Hand | Lantern tower hour before reading. Feng's 400 vols, index in head, degrading hand (plot fact). Ning Wu not in furnace column but in fourth column with no heading. Feng copies in one sitting, hand worsening. Hope in bad handwriting. Pull: kiln road/sealed door. |
| 18 | The Kiln Road | Lowwater to Broken Kiln last four miles. Sect watch, Warden's marker on post (same Vol 4). Wen cart stops at marker — unable not unwilling, says Not here. Kiln sealed by layer not masonry; Lantern cannot cross. Ning walks last miles alone, arrives after dark with hour light. Terror. Pull: seal stops Yuan not body. |
| 19 | Sealed Ground | Broken Kiln yard after dark. Goal assessment room. Building ruin; crossing threshold Yuan silent first time — absent not blocked, returns count wrong. Seal is instruction, he is inside issuer. She goes in alone 40 min, only truly-alone time Vol 1, metallic taste. Reversal: reliance gone where needed. Pull: room itself. |
| 20 | The Assessment Room | Assessment room, clay by year no names out. Finds Ning Wu: *assessed, reassigned, unreconciled* exact wording. Kiln evaluates not processes; fourth column is reassigned. Copies date on knee in dark hour, hands nearly dead. Not relief — brother worth something. Revelation, first real news six years. Pull: procedure wall. |
| 21 | What They Take | Procedure wall in Bureau hand for those who agree. Kiln manufactures: stalled opened, useful part moved into failing, both filed same number. Furnace supply chain. Tablet single fired plate size of hand, surface like skin (once, remembered Vol 16). Decides aloud to empty room — irreversible, to building, nobody listening. 40 min before marker reports weight. |
| 22 | The Weight at the Marker | Out of kiln down four miles. Seal logs her; layer notes somewhere unseen. Yuan back: cannot undo, cannot tell cost. Tablet first thing out sealed place 16 years. She keeps walking, does not ask pay/he does not offer — ledger now. 7→8 via carry/no-sleep, tax untouched noticed. Settlement rises. Cost: both keep books. Has tablet, no one to show. Layer bracket: removal logged. |
| 23 | Wen Again | Lowwater lamp yard two days later. Wants tablet read. Wen will not touch — professional disgust, specific. Reveals tablet made of people, core-stone is fired qi. Ning does not put down; wears against skin, never comfortable rest Vol 1. Evidence → person. Cost: hold not show. Pull: one person untested (voice through hand). |
| 24 | The Tablet | Outer yard weekday morning. Showing = capital admission, not showing = carry piece forever. Tries voice: Yuan holds through her — first time in 19 years he picks anything up. She forbids twice. Reads herself, writes log in own hand clerks cannot read quickly — first thing for herself. No growth (8 holds). Settlement spikes, loses mother's name (name itself, grief remains, never again). Discovery: log is weapon. Pull: Qiu waiting at steps. |

## Batch 0004 (ch 37–46)

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 37 | Stage Four | Laundry room and crew quarters, three days. She says nothing to him for three days to test whether the channel needs her: it does not. Fear comes off him as heat for the first time. In an unattempted argument about coal she keeps him in four lines — he is the only person who has lied to her in a way she could check. Account open, not closed: employed, not forgiven. She asks what he took, item by item, and he can only answer in weather. |
| 38 | The Scar | Furnace floor, night shift, week two. She asks what is under the scar. He describes the withdrawal as a thickness, a stack, a shelf. She finishes it: you did not take a thing, you took the order the thing was in. The tax takes order, not substance — practice rebuilds stages and cannot rebuild order. He does not understand the reading of himself. She tells him to stop describing things in images, because she is going to start reading what he reads. Intimacy in its worst form. |
| 39 | Eleven Days | Ash-flats, eleven days, stage 4 to 7. The yard refuses her the inner ring; Mei Jiu warns her once from the rail and goes inside. The leak through the crack costs her a fifth of everything she moves until she watches it go sideways into the Nine-Step pattern instead of out, and the pattern does work. Damage as technique, hers and unshared, and she tells nobody. Body-Forging 7 announced bound-wide to a terrace that has stopped being surprised. Four days left and the last technique is the one he cannot describe. |
| 40 | Feng Lushi Writes | Lowwater, the tower, a day. The Bureau has ordered Feng to omit her from the upward copy; he has disobeyed for eight days and has written her story upward four times in an unindexed column nobody reads, because he has never had a story where somebody was believed. She refuses to thank him and he says that is how it is done. She asks for the order of the sentences and he gives her eleven volumes without knowing it is a method. Stage 8 on the Lowwater road under forty-one volumes. The Watch is waiting at the foot of the terrace. |
| 41 | Lowwater | Held in the roundhouse on a Bureau note charging her with removal of kiln material, which is the wrong clause. Old Gao's hand on a young Watchman's arm stops a search that would have found the tablet. Captain Lu Tao walks her out in daylight. In the market square she reads a log page aloud and the crowd turns on the paper, not on her; part of it wants to know what she took out of the kiln and she refuses to read the name in public. She has leverage for the first time and gives the captain up instead of finishing him. It tastes like Yuan. |
| 42 | Three Counties | The copying floor at the fourth watch. Four sheets, one hour, four genuinely different sentences: Lowwater's plain report, Hanhui's *for discipline* with a county stamp, Shouyi's with her name removed, Wanzhou's *a servant of Qinghe*. The copyists reproduce faithfully; they were not in the room. The layer is written by somebody. She takes the three foreign sheets and puts them in the log. Her brother's sentence is now a piece of furniture in an office. She asks the voice who writes them and he says he has never known. |
| 43 | What the Voice Knows | Grain cart up the Lowwater road. He can see the sentences and cannot see where any of them is decided. She discovers he is counted once a day by something walking a row in the layer; he has assumed for six years he is a fault in the sky and now there are people in the room. He is more frightened than she is. She refuses to comfort him and tells him she needs him sharp, which is the first time she has needed him. She reaches stage 9 on a terrace step at dusk by opening herself on purpose, unaided. |
| 44 | The Terms of the Hearing | Corridor outside the Assembly Hall, day before the Hall closes the season. The Hall tries not to rule; clerk Xie Shuo forces it, because a non-ruling travels upward. He rules there is no precedent and then rules anyway, and rules that the Hall's own objection is the writ for her seal. Qiu offers his sponsor's writ and she refuses it in front of eleven witnesses, because a favour is revocable. She petitions for a public advancement hearing and for the Assembly floor and for nine hundred witnesses. Nine days to object. |
| 45 | Nine Days | The nine days on the flats, on her back. A list of four things going wrong and a stage nine that is a ceiling with a hole in it. Qiu's faction sends the second offer by servant-rank clerk in a corridor: inner rank, bed, stipend, account struck, Foundation, the tablet — for a signature that she has never entered sealed ground and never carried kiln clay. She does not burn it. She logs it word for word with the date, the clerk and the pin hole. Sealed for Condensing on the eighth day with three witnesses: the first servant-born in three hundred years. |
| 46 | The Petition | Assembly Hall, nine hundred and eleven people, the winter accounts take four hours and put her father's account and her name on the floor again. The Hall tries to defer her petition to the close of the session; she forces the reading on a rule she learned from the worst clerk she has ever met. An objection from Qiu's faction is read and disposed of on its own last line. Her name is read aloud for the second time in the volume. She petitions that the layer speak and wins it on the four county sentences. Trial set for the Assembly floor in four days, announced to the bound. |

## Batch 0005 (ch 47–58) — closes Volume 1

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 47 | Waiting | Crew quarters, day one of the eleven-day ash fall. Ash in the lye trough, on the sill, on the washing crew's backs, in her lungs; her stair climb now costs her. Yuan tells her the real clock: a sealed member who does not attempt on the floor is recorded as a *refusal*, and a refusal is an intake, not a defeat. Mei Jiu comes to her door in the dark and hands her an unwritten inner compression for a narrow gate, for nothing, with no explanation, because she would rather the Hall be frightened of what happens if Ning Yan is refused than compete with her. Ning Yan refuses to write it in the log, so that a compression nobody has written down cannot be found in a Bureau hand. Xie Shuo asks to be one of the nine floor clerks. A folded sheet arrives from the Hall: the sponsorship terms, before the trial. |
| 48 | Terms | West corridor, day two. Lu Kang, Deng Chao and Pei An offer inner rank at first sitting, Foundation outside the furnace schedule, the account of Ning Bo struck, the Clay Tablet, and a clause barring her from ever mentioning the voice — with a penalty that reinstates everything rather than a fine. Third sheet reveals nine households struck in one brush stroke over eleven years: the faction has been running its own furnace out of Qiu's rooms and has never had to produce a word. She asks for one clause and they agree in four seconds, which is the horror. She nearly signs, stops on the thought that a bought truth is not a truth, and leaves the sheet on the table with the hour blank. Three days to decide. |
| 49 | Withheld | Anteroom, day three. She asks to sign after the trial; Lu Kang explains, without cruelty, that an advancement with no paper in front of it belongs to the sect, and one with paper belongs to the men who put her on the floor — the fourth hour and the ninth hour are different in law. She sits eight hours in a room where the waiting is the point, manages a cough in public, and says no. The offer dies at the last hour of the day, schedule of nine struck out with it; the minute-book entry about her brother survives because a minute book is not a schedule. At the second hour of the night the same servant-rank clerk walks six miles in the rain to bring the sheet back with no hour, no term and no deadline: they worked out in six minutes that a dated offer is an offer she is holding. She notices she has not said *please* once in eight hours. |
| 50 | Qiu's Fall | Assembly Hall, dawn of the trial, heaviest day of the ash fall, doors propped because the hinges have packed. Qiu tells her about a lamp shop in Lowwater and *the shape of a happy life*, and says he has been at the furnace forty-one years trying to buy it out of other people's children. The Hall vacates the entry of the eleventh day and eleven minutes as retrospective — and Qiu refuses to plead it, because pleading would void her rank with his. He is stripped in four minutes, pensioned, silent for the rest of the morning. On his way out he slows half a step beside her and says the sentence nobody understands, including the reader. She lets him go past and says nothing, and understands for the first time what it is to be inside a thing happening to a person with no way to touch it. Her name is called. |
| 51 | The Floor | Assembly Hall floor, ninth hour. She counts the room through Yuan: four people have decided something and one of them is the clerk. She starts with the Nine-Step and her body is four steps ahead of her head; she refuses, aloud, on a floor, to explain where it came from, and does not tell Yuan either. Mei Jiu's compression narrows the crack to a rim and works, and she states plainly that it is not hers and that she will look at Mei Jiu while she uses it so the room knows where to send the bill. Her right hand fails at the sixth form and she does not stop. Terror becomes the enormous specific relief of doing the one thing she is good at. Then the air falls out of the room and the ash goes straight down. |
| 52 | The Tribulation | The same room, immediately. The weather has a number in it — five — because a trial is a document and the bound executes it rather than reading it. The Speaker orders the floor cleared and cannot move her off it. She is struck five times: left forearm, right arm, a beam and the roof taken out, and the fourth out of a sky the building has not seen in three hundred years. She does not get up for two minutes and forty seconds. On the fifth she refuses to take a shape and opens the crack outward instead — Condensing stage one, the aperture stabilised at the crack as a mouth that will open and shut for the rest of her life. The Hall has no roof and never gets one again. |
| 53 | The Record of the Trial | Immediately after. Du Hong lifts her onto the Speaker's bench without asking and she lets him, knowing the report order he was sent with and that he does not know she knows. Xie Shuo writes for a minute and forty seconds and then stops, and four seconds pass in which the entire hall waits for a sound that does not come — the visible edge of a decision, and she looks for the cause in every corner of the room and finds nothing in it at all, then goes looking inside and finds him not saying anything. The announcement goes out and carries **servant-born**, which in eleven hundred years it has never once done; the word goes round the hall in about nine hundred mouths. Announcement wording fixed here: *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born. What was held is let go. What was taken is held.* |
| 54 | What It Cost Him | Sixth step of the terrace, hours later, in the debris. He has been silent for nineteen minutes and she asks and he explains: a report is made and then released, and the release is the act of whoever is holding it, which is why no advancement has ever waited. If he had held it, the register says a sealed member did not attempt, and a furnace looks for exactly that. He chose the announcement over her safety because a servant-born who announces is too public to harvest quietly. He does not say how long he held it; she works out from the rule alone that it was the four seconds and neither of them says the number out loud. The layer has logged him and has a name for him: **Yuan Sheng**. Settlement passes two-thirds; Ledger is offered for ninety seconds and refused, and he loses the ability to read past her skin permanently. She does not forgive him, sets the rule that he decides nothing about her again without telling her, and stays. |
| 55 | Three Days Visible | Three days in the crew quarters, which she refuses to leave for a better room. Fever gone; right arm gone. Three factions inside a day want something from her. Her father's account is opened again in **four** county books, each of which adds *Credit. Condensing, stage one. Held* and bills the household twice more — being famous has cost her father eight more years. The layer enters **the first claim in the history of the bound against the Chosen One**, eight hundred jin, proof defined as a body, a place and a name; she cannot refuse it and he did not do it and he made her one. Three people come believing her and every one wants something; the laundress teaches her that being believed is the only one of the three resources that can be spent, and she spends it — eight words, her own hand, sent to the Lantern Register tower with nobody's name on it. Rage arrives over a Bureau copyist who cannot get *servant-born* right — the first straight thing she has felt in four months. **Then she spends it.** Du Hong finds her on the sixth step and she tells him the thing she has held since before the trial: he was sent to report her, he filed nothing, and she has never once mentioned it in any room, which is the only reason nobody has asked him why. He tells her it cannot be held open — with a price on her head he is the cheapest door in the bound. She says she will put the whole of it on the record in her own hand, and he tells her that will put her name in his file, and she says yes, because that is how she makes herself expensive. "Mostly at you." The protection and the danger are the same line in the same four books. |
| 56 | The Signature | Roofless Assembly Hall, fifth day after the trial, forty-one witnesses, the crew standing in the weather. She enters a schedule of the route to the Broken Kiln in her own readable hand and then states, under oath, that the article has not been in her possession since the hour she left sealed ground — a positive falsehood, said to their faces, witnessed, and the first thing she has ever done that is not strictly true. She reads her brother's record aloud in a room with no roof, twice, and the clerk enters one word. She signs the clause barring her from ever mentioning the voice, knowing it cannot bind a Chosen One and that it binds her by hand. Account of Ning Bo struck whole; the maintaining members' iron token given. **Then she uses the standing she has just bought.** She goes back in and has a second entry taken on the same sheet in her own hand: Du Hong advanced to inner rank at the winter session on her recommendation, directed by the Hall to report her, filed nothing, and the fact that she has held that since before the trial and entered nothing until this hour. Lu Kang lets it stand because a maintaining member may dictate from the hour she signs. The cost is immediate and she does it anyway — two files with her hand in them, so any man cashing eight hundred jin opens him first. She cannot afterwards tell whether she was angry or shopping. Relief enormous, and not the same as knowing. The Chosen One bounty and the word *reconciled* are now on the same line of the same four books. |
| 57 | The Shape of the Silence | Ninth Steps Terrace, ninth day of the ash, first grey snow. She goes up all nine steps for the first time since the winter. Yuan gives her what he has: the designation is a writ and not a reward; there have been **eleven** before her across six hundred years and he cannot see past any of the seams; and a designation can be *set aside* by a request from outside the bound, by somebody he cannot name. She stops him mid-sequence — *not tonight, you have spent enough of me tonight* — and for the first time in four months it works. She keeps him: employed, not forgiven, three rules, the work is her brother. She uses his name once, wrongly, and says she will be wrong again. He goes quiet for four hours without warning. |
| 58 | The Count | Same day, the terrace in the first snow. Du Hong brings her two lamp-menders' versions of her own announcement, both wrong in different ways, and she understands it is not carelessness but ears; she sends the four county sheets down to Lowwater in copies in her own hand. Two men are on the gate who were not on it when she came up, and one of them asked Du Hong what his file said before he had got to the fourth step: the entry she made in ch 56 has already made him somebody to stop. The pass closes and she never gets off the mountain. He comes back after three hours and gives her a number: **fifty-eight**, one a day since the first night — which means the row that walks past him has been coming down since before she ever existed and has been checking an empty slot, or something older than her. There is a place where the sentences are read after they go out, and it has a name he has never once been able to read, and it is the only thing in the layer he cannot see. She stands on the sixth step with the token she does not want and asks what it is counting. He does not answer. He is counting. |

## Volume 2 — The Chosen One (ch 59–118)

### Batch 0006 (ch 59–72) — opens Volume 2

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 59 | The Closed Pass | Tenth day of the fall, second hour. The pass at the fourth bridge iced over between the second and the fourth hour and Captain Lu Tao has the road barred; in his own hand, with no order on it, his barrier board reads *Ning Yan. Held.* He tells her why: a claim of eight hundred jin stands in the layer against the Chosen One and there is one person inside this wall who is that name. She counts to forty, finds nothing on the list the ice can fix, and turns round and walks back up all nine steps and takes the second bunk **on purpose, in front of the crew**, converting Volume 1's visibility into confinement. The barrier is a schedule, not a punishment. Power: none. |
| 60 | The Second Bunk | Eleventh and last day of the ash. She tells eleven men the true part — the dead arm, the crack as a door that does not shut properly, the eight hundred jin — and keeps the plate and the log to herself, because eleven men is not a lock. Cao Lei (back stair, autumn) and Xu An (oil ration, autumn) find the shape of it: **the roster takes a signature, any man of the crew can sign for a shift, and a signed shift is a legal fact regardless of who works it.** Stated once, here, by Cao Lei in the quarters. She lets them, after telling them aloud what a roster is, so the cover carries a price. Last day of the fall; it stops by evening. At the second night hour a clerk's folded sheet waits on the Hall table with her name on it in the wrong order. **Nothing on this page foretells what it costs.** |
| 61 | The Licence Clerk | Road opens for market. **She puts ch 60's folded sheet on the clerk's table first.** It came off the Hall table at the west end with the queue papers; his is a copyist's hand — *not a hand that writes names, a hand that writes down what it has been given.* She tells him to write her name and **he writes it in the wrong order**, because he has no other version. An office has her name down wrong and there is now a copy of the error in a building with no roof. Then the Hall's new clerk, eleven days at the table and more afraid of the three sponsors than of her, reads out **Clause Four of the Servant Advancement Settlement** and names the document: the **Licence to Practise Past Registration**, issued at the **Bureau of Records' county office at Lowwater**, registrar **Sun He** named once. Three parts and no part is the licence: **sponsors' seal, quota clearance, county countersignature.** She owns one and does not even have that yet. Quota clearance is counted in **hours**, not weeks, and the hours go into Shen Xiu's winter book. She copies the clause into the log in wash code and resolves to file. |
| 62 | The Price of Paper | The sponsors' corridor, sixth hour of the evening. Lu Kang, Deng Chao and Pei An seal the application — and the clause they drafted themselves in the second week of the autumn falls due at noon: **a fired plate the size of a hand returned to the maintaining members within the week of signature.** It cannot be late, because she swore in her own hand at the second hour and thirty-one minutes of the fifth day that she cannot produce it and does not undertake to. The paper demands an article the only person who could produce it has sworn she has not got. What they want is the schedule worked and her mouth shut, in that order, and she notices herself bargaining while it happens. Sealing enters her name in the county queue: **a public fact.** |
| 63 | The Lowwater Market | Old Gao holds her at the trestle by name, politely, *for her own safety* — the bounty made courteous — and will carry a letter up. It comes the next morning with the carts: **Du Hong delivered the four county copies to the tower, Feng read all four, walled them behind the fifth item, and entered none of them upward.** Feng's unindexed column stays at five and he has said *do not thank me for it.* The rest of the letter is the new fact: a man has been to **three county desks on the Lowwater square, not the tower**, asking which clerk keeps the copies of the Qinghe advancement, and has been paying for the answer. **The bounty has moved from wordings to records.** She writes back one line in her own unreadable hand — *copy nothing for him* — and works out that the third proof is a body, and therefore a room. **Pressed on the claim, Yuan fails to give her the arithmetic and says why: it does not come out of a ledger or a list, it comes out of a man asked in daylight who has had to answer in front of people who will remember it, and he will not say it from above a crowd.** *Ask me when there is one.* That is ch 68. |
| 64 | The Furnace Tithe | Night shift, one-handed. The floor does not know her after eleven days. The shift leader pities her onto the bench, which is worse than contempt, and she refuses and holds the channel instead. **The crack-as-channel is used, not explained** (spent at ch 39) and nobody on the floor sees a technique. The tithe table is four feet from the furnace mouth and the clerk counts slowly **on purpose**, because a fast number makes the book unbalanced at month's end. She signs the tithe book with her left hand for **four and a half hours**, entering herself as labour toward her own clearance. **New:** the sheet above the columns carries six unsigned lines saying the hours a furnace hand works are hours the county counts at the countersignature, delivered on the fourth day past, with eleven other unsigned sheets in a frame at the quota office. Held for Volume 7; not used again this volume. |
| 65 | Bunk by Bunk | Eighth hour of the morning. Two officers of the maintaining members come up the quarters stair with a genuine instrument for an examination of crew effects after the trial debris inventory. **The maintaining member's consent regulation, stated once and never explained again:** her effects may not be examined without her consent, and consent may be withheld without reason and cannot be challenged. She withholds it at the eighth hour. At the same hour Cao Lei and Xu An **stand up in front of eleven witnesses and claim her shift aloud**, which gives her a legal hour off the floor — and she spends it at the Hall getting the furnace schedule certified, unattached, filing nothing. **The clerk certifies her hours for the eleventh to the seventeenth as four and one-half**, the rest being the two men who sign for her. Gratitude with teeth: they have given the inspectors a line to write down, and she tells them so. **The roster rule is not restated here — it was stated once in the quarters at ch 60, and she remembers that room, not a book.** |
| 66 | The Inspectors' Paper | Tenth hour through midday; the doorway refusal is entered at **ten minutes past the tenth hour**. **The batch's irreversible decision.** She refuses the examination again, aloud, in the doorway, before eleven witnesses, and enters the hour twice. The seal holds. **The inspectors take the crew roster instead** — a board is sect property, an instrument silent on a board takes it, and they read it aloud against the certified schedule in front of the foreman, and in eleven seconds a short-handed man who says he is **short of two, and the two of them are standing on his stair** puts two names on the settling floor doubled from tomorrow to the close of the thaw. She has four moves available and uses none: she could not say *take me instead*, because of the plate. **She chose two lives that were not hers over one life that was.** A younger inspector, packing up, tells her kindly that they did not come for anything she is wearing, and that the maintaining members already hold the plate described in their own hand — **which tells her the clause at ch 62 was written for this, and by whom.** The option of surrendering the plate quietly closes. The refusal is in the minute book and retrievable in Volume 4. |
| 67 | Ash and Hours | Tithe table, seventh hour of the evening. The clerk is beginning to enjoy the counting and she notices and does not like it. Above her head Cao Lei and Xu An are on the settling floor, cheerful, arguing the second verse of the weighing song so the floor can hear. She reads a debt paper upside down over the clerk's tray: **Hou Sanliang, of Lowwater, creditor on three of the nine struck households, striking dates eleven months and eleven days apart — the same spacing, to the day, as the sponsors' eleven-year schedule — with a fourth line citing the maintaining members' ledger by name.** She has two minutes and the whole room's attention is elsewhere and she **says nothing and counts her hours**. Restraint as action. **Her second shift of four and a half hours goes in, and the clerk reads her week back at nine.** |
| 68 | The Bounty Man | Third hour past midday, the outer yard, forty witnesses. A well-dressed man of about fifty, polite, unhurried, asks the gate clerk **aloud and in daylight** for the place where the Chosen One sleeps and the name on the carriers' papers — **two of three proofs, bought in public, with a coin on the table so the clerk is not left to guess whether he is being paid.** She gets four seconds of his face and uses three. She says *read him*; Yuan tells her flat that he cannot past her skin, and that reaching would mean taking something back he has not named, and that the spike **is paid out of her foundation and does not come back**, and that he has about four months. **Ledger's price is stated once here and is not restated at ch 80.** She does not order it. **Here, and once only, he gives the arithmetic she was refused at ch 63:** a claim in the bound is entered by the layer against something, and **a man who works an entered act is working inside it**, and she does not say the word and is asked to and refuses. Neither of them says *listed* aloud. Fury, clean and straight, the batch's first. |
| 69 | The Quota Table | Sixth hour of the morning through a three-hour wait in an anteroom where nobody looks at her, which turns out to be the most useful half hour of her winter. **The frame holds twelve unsigned sheets. Two of them are in the roundhouse hand of Captain Lu Tao, whose barrier board read *Ning Yan. Held.* in the same hand at ch 59** — she writes that into the log. Shen Xiu does not refuse her; he puts her household's line on the table: **hours asked 940, hours worked 211, standing 729, and 940 is a furnace crew's year to the last ten hours.** Clearance is arithmetic, not favour, and not his; a sponsor with a sheet and a seal moves it nothing. He will not weight the asked figure for her arm. **Clearance is two numbers: her line level at the close of the year, and the winter book closing square — and the book is out by 611 hours on five estates that are not hers, so the whole of the gap she has to close is her own 729.** **Twenty-three days of thaw will not do it. Middle of spring for the certificate, first week of summer for the county to receive it.** He refuses to hurry it and has already told two Elders what happens if he does, and refuses to tell her. She pleads nothing, because there is nothing to say. Something close to respect for a man she cannot forgive. |
| 70 | The Refusal Filed | Ninth hour of the morning, the Hall landing, nine people in a queue in the weather. A clerk out of Lowwater reads Clause Four aloud with no expression. **Refused for want of quota clearance**, with the sponsors' seal already on the paper above the refusal and visible to every name in the queue. The refusal wording is fixed here for the rest of the volume: *Application refused. Quota clearance not produced. The sponsors' seal is of no effect without it and is not opened. A further application may be made upon production of the said clearance. The application is returned entire.* She asks for the county to be named on it and is told she is the first in two years to ask. **She files once.** She keeps the slip, does not tear it, and logs the wording. Then she works the cold end and looks up at a slag beam with a hairline in the near shoring, because you look up first at a cold end. |
| 71 | The Left Hand's Work | Ninth hour of the night, the twenty-first day. The melt has got into the channel, four men are in a hole that needs six, and the foreman signed the shoring over on the seventh hour. **The beam comes down on the doubled shift and both men are dead before the healer reaches the floor, forty minutes later.** A **girl of twenty-four on the cold end** goes off the lip on the same movement with her leg gone one way, and Ning Yan sits with the leg above the break until the healer comes down for it. She gets a nine-foot slag beam up two inches off Xu An on her own for eleven seconds and then has to leave it because the channel is about to come over and there are **eleven** men on the floor below her; she holds the channel with the crack open until the fourth night hour and **fourteen** people are counted off at the top of a stair. **Yuan is useless and says so in the first second** — he cannot lift it, and reaching would take it out of her foundation, and that is the whole of it. She washes their faces before she can speak. [The furnace at Qinghe. Two of the crew are ended beneath a fallen beam.] **From the ninth hour of the morning the crew is nine, and every count after this reads nine.** |
| 72 | Two Names in the Entry | Fifth day of the thaw; first hard frost back. The sect buries furnace dead in nineteen minutes and does not ask for causes. She gives them in the tally voice, both of them, in front of **nine** men: **the roster was taken because she refused the examination of her bunk**, and **the doubled shift existed because two crew marks stood against her hours**. Yuan offers to put the beam on a different shelf from the refusal and she will not allow it. The burial clerk enters nine lines including hers, unasked. **She writes the burial register entry in her own left hand** — the first document she has ever written that names her as the cause of anything — and ends it *and that the undersigned caused both of these things*. It records the refusals as the eighth hour and the tenth hour of the sixteenth day. Xu An's mother tells her she will not be at her door. She puts the refused licence slip under the cover of the tithe book with the three seals showing, so that in about nine weeks a quota clerk counts **nine hours** against a member and finds county paper under his wrist. **Cao Lei's wage is six and eight a week and thirteen weeks of it is undrawn, and an unsettled column is about to take it.** The girl of twenty-four is at the wash house with a lath and a cord for a splint and the season off the settling floor, charged to a household that is not hers. Grief enacted as labour. |

### Batch 0007 (ch 73–88) — the grinding batch, volume midpoint reversal

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 73 | The Tithe Ledger | Day 23, morning after burial. She puts the tithe back to work on a floor short two men; foreman posts five where six belong, promises six on the board. Signs two blocks (13.5 in her own hand); household 229/711. Fetches the dead man's quarter-wage off his father's column; nods to the splinted girl at the wash house. |
| 74 | What the Body Owes | One-arm channel work, eleven-minute window, leak as cold stripe, iron in the throat. Rebuilds rhythm eleven minutes left; clerk counts slow on purpose. Household 247/693; her line 18. Girl copies carriers' lists, holds the bunk secret. |
| 75 | The Wash House Splint | Corrects a four-hour carrier error on the girl's slate; difference charged to eighth estate, not hers. Girl heard the gate man ask where Ning Yan sleeps and gave nothing. No rescue, no thanks; she works the next block. Household 265/675; her line 22.5. |
| 76 | The Escort She Does Not Have | Needs lawful descent with a claim standing; a guard is a place. **Arranges it only** — gate clerk, Old Gao at the trestle, Watch to be consulted, cart sheet at the tenth hour. Du Hong's letter: buyer pays at three county desks for records; tower loft has a mat. **She does not board** — stands at the trestle at the tenth hour and lets the water go down without her. Household 283/657; her line 27. |
| 77 | The Left-Hand Habit | **Stays on the mountain**: cold yard behind the furnace, second bunk in the crew quarters. Rebuilds left-hand form from Nine-Step habit (unwritten, unshared) plus crack-channel practice in the cold; carries plate stacks because the crew is short two. Cart goes down three cleared days without her. Five and a half weeks of tithe behind it; breath shortens to two counts. Household 301/639; her line 31.5. Edge near. |
| 78 | What the Bound Is Told | Condensing 1 → 2 on the settling floor, finished the hour first. Yuan lets the announcement go without her order; she is furious, does not stop him, does not thank him. One bracketed layer line, four-clause order with Condensing, stage two, at Qinghe; not the ch 53 wording. Cost: two weeks fever, month of short breath never fully leaving. |
| 79 | Work Through Fever | Twelve of fourteen fever days worked; bench as tactics not pity. Clerk shows the running line; household 355/585; her line 45. Breath stays short. No second accident, no rescue. |
| 80 | Read Him Aloud | Batch climax. Good coat back at the gate asking two proofs aloud. She orders read him; Yuan holds Ledger less than a day, reads the employed man through her eyes (broker, counting room, records not wordings, **which household she is entered against; a body is not to be asked for, only waited on**), pays the spike to about six and a half weeks, refuses to hold it again same day and permanently. Price paid, not restated. Hers the only order. Gate clerk says the asking is now in a file. **No log entry and no tithe figure here — both belong to ch 81.** |
| 81 | The Counting Room's Employee | **Still on the mountain.** Logs the reading in wash code on the Clause Four docket, adds the sixth mark. Letter down to Du Hong: copy nothing for him. Du Hong's letter up with the water reports the surety terms (living name, death voids, debt transfers) and that a clerk asked what he carries and has a file on it; he writes them down first so nobody can say nobody told her. She rides down at the week's close. Household 412/528; her line 58. Hou not met. |
| 82 | The Barrier Escort | **The arrival, and the first time in Lowwater mud.** Down to stay on day 58 on the water cart sheet, tenth hour, daylight, Watch walking the rail. Tower loft, mat, stair once. Copying floor vs county office held distinct; Feng column five, four copies walled, none upward. Stands in the square at night and works out what the county wants: a name that dies before she does — too late to stop it, Du Hong stands while she is four miles up a frozen pass. Household 468/472; her line 72. On the ground. |
| 83 | Weight of the Market | Carrying loads past three desks; buyer polite, pays for writing as well as answers, place unbought. Counting room stacked by household, employed man reports. **New danger: he buys the absence** — a page in a county file saying no household in this town has her entered against it, worth more than her address. Household 520/420; her line 85. No confrontation. |
| 84 | Nights in the Tower Loft | Copying floor: four copies behind fifth item, entered none upward, column five, no audit, no Lung. Sleeps in loft, carries tower loads; shutter fixed by Du Hong. Asks Feng what paper has her name on it: intake schedule as labour only, the mat entered nowhere, and anything he writes goes up the stair. The county is the only room that will put a line under her. Decides to go up on the dawn cart. Household 610/330; her line 102. Shelter not safety. |
| 85 | The Office on the Square | County office inquiry with clearance unearned: three legs, standing voids them. **He does not read the lesser paper's terms to a hand that has not cleared** — only that it exists, that it is given to hands that work, that it is made of hours, and that in nineteen years three of four he handed it out came back signed. **The full terms are read for the first time at ch 88.** County office vs tower held distinct. Household 700/240; her line 128. Does not file. |
| 86 | Clearance Without a Look | Day 79: household 940/940, book square including five estates not hers. Shen Xiu signs looking past her, no cruelty, tired, honest; costs every hour worked and the days not worked. Walks under the sound timber to the settling floor holding it; the two dead and the girl are the cost the book does not keep. No plea, no thanks, no forgiveness. Second leg owned. |
| 87 | The Name for Attendance | Loft, then the sponsors' corridor: silent on the dead Foundation writ (reaches only through a licence); three men knew and said nothing. Du Hong tells her the terms, says he wants his name to be the whole line of a county book, and stands in the office aloud: living surety, death voids, debt transfers, entered. Application complete: seal, clearance, surety. |
| 88 | The Pending Filing | Volume midpoint reversal, irreversible hers. Certain of licence; Sun He refuses on the bounty standing (licenced practitioner cannot stand under unanswered claim). Reads pending filing; she enters aperture cracked through; stabilised at the crack; permanent in her own hand, signs ceiling at Condensing 5, barred Foundation without relicensing. Writ dies on same page (Foundation on it is unregistered practice under a schedule it does not appear on). Refused and signed same hour. On the ground at Lowwater. |

### Batch 0008 (ch 89–103) — pursuit and the loss

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 89 | The Filing Copy | First week of pending life: county hours, stamp days, tower readings attended with Du Hong; carrying stone, oil, paper, water under schedule; buyer still paying at three desks, reporting to counting room. **The good coat steps aside of the county door and makes a courtesy — a small man deciding not to follow.** She does the county's sum against the schedule's: the county wants days she stood in a room, the schedule wants loads, **nineteen weeks, all of them attendable, and she decides yes without writing it down.** Filing holds because she stands where book says. |
| 90 | The Man Who Pays for Writing | Hunt moves from wordings to records to hours: buyer pays for asking and writing, wants hours beside carriers' names; younger man appears, walks lanes. **She watches the first desk clerk's face while he writes and files that he wrote slowly and put his brush down flat — a man ending a thing he would rather not have been asked to do.** Strap cuts her palm; she wraps it in her lining and keeps carrying. She tells Du Hong to stand where she stands tomorrow and correct anyone who writes him wrong; he learns in one exchange what she already knew — that his hours sit beside hers on a page a man could buy. |
| 91 | Four Weeks of Stone | Three weeks of load building toward edge: stone, oil, paper, water; left-hand sequence drilled nightly; arm holds third and stays; breath two counts. **Two stamp days named and watched — a third column struck whole and nine households transferred at county pace, which is how she learns the county can empty a household out of a book.** **The girl of twenty-four corrects her four hours to two in person: the cart goes at the seventh and returns at the eleventh, and she is copying it fair for the eighth estate so nobody answers for it twice.** She decides to take stage three at her order, and works out that an unannounced stage would leave the filing counting hours like a book with a page torn out that nobody had asked for. |
| 92 | Where She Sleeps | Condensing 2→3 at her order, announced bound-wide. One bracket, four-clause order with stage three at Lowwater. **She checks the right arm during the stage — it comes back whole, weighs nothing, and is the most frightening part of the night; the leak works without being asked for the first time.** The announcement names the tower as well as the town, and three towers in Lowwater keep books and one keeps a column at five. **Du Hong's one sentence: every one of these is a line with her name at the end of it and he is standing on all of them.** She has it entered before the stamp, not after. Arm stays third. She attends stamp day known. |
| 93 | The Safe Side | Lie part one: being Chosen is why claim cannot take her quietly; eleven across six hundred years, designation reason for record; protection and danger same line, safe side. Writ not reward stands. **She makes him give the figure and he refuses — a man who will not give you the number knows the number is the wrong size for the question.** **She admits the shameful part: from the terrace on, some part of her had been waiting for him to say the claim could not touch her, because it was easier to owe your life to a voice than to a schedule.** Eleven bodies is a number and a number does not care who is speaking it. She does not agree aloud. |
| 94 | Eleven Bodies | She tests the sentence against a person who is not her: **the girl of twenty-four, asked directly whether it is safe, answers that she is the safest thing in this town and has been since a winter she does not describe.** She learns the county hand writes large and the tower hand writes small, and that a person in a hurry takes whichever is nearer the street — **the tower is the copy, the county is the record.** **Sun He gives her the longest sentence he has given her and it is not kindness: the filing only fails when it is broken, so keep it unbroken until spring.** Overhearing the counting room: **nine households walked, two yes, neither about her — her name is one line in a ledger of nine that has been running since the autumn.** Holds the sentence without using it. |
| 95 | What Walks Behind Coin | Pursuit to bodies: younger man walks behind carriers, asks after Du Hong by description, pays for hours and lanes. **Seen from the intake shed roof: he buys a schedule and then buys *there is a woman sets the loads down slow*. He has her by her pace, not her name.** **She moves her tower intake line from the fifth hour to the ninth — entered by Feng as a tower change and not a request, because a request carries a reason and a change carries a sheet — and knows it goes up the same stair as everything else.** Feng: make the counting expensive. Counting room behind both; Hou never met. Settlement thinning to weeks. |
| 96 | Kiln-Born | Paper turn: tower volumes status column, eleven entries six hundred years, dead hand; nearest before hers says *kiln-born*; Lowwater sheet same word second position no other county has; one of four copies carries it. **Four of the nine ranks of years stand out of nine — four volumes opened often enough that the oilcloth has gone back at the corners, all left open at the same page, because that is what a hand does when it comes back to a thing it wants. And an eleventh line ruled across the page in hard pencil with nothing in it, waiting for hers, ruled before the sponsorship and before the claim.** She does not open a page and does not stop dusting. **A living clerk in Lowwater wrote the word into a county sheet out of a book six hundred years old.** Spoken once to Yuan; unrecognised — and a man in the bound since before the county kept books not knowing it means it is only in the paper. |
| 97 | A Word With Nobody | She carries the word without spending it: writes nothing of it, asks Feng nothing, attends the stamp day. **The cost of talking to the only hand in the world that could find out is not that he would tell — it is that a man who has been asked small true things begins to wait for the large one.** She looks at the fifth rank, where Feng's own column lives, and does not ask. Held in two heads, no paper; column five; Feng unaudited, no Lung. |
| 98 | The Cheap Door | Pending holds under pursuit at the cost of visibility and a name beside hers: they attend together through the void-and-transfer reading. **She watches a counting-room woman have four households read out and priced and walk out with nothing in her hands and no hand on her arm — and understands she has been afraid of the wrong thing for four months; the bound prices a household and lets it find out later, and a body is the last thing in the process and the easiest to refuse.** **Du Hong admits his hours are on a clerk's page and that he let it happen on purpose, having told no one — and puts it in daylight before he uses it.** Settlement near days. |
| 99 | Five Days of Margin | Count: about five days of ordinary margin; Ledger refused permanently, nothing ordered, no trial standing anywhere, and the only thing left that settles a Settlement is a listed act ended by her hand — which cannot be arranged and cannot be bought. **He refuses to give her the argument she came up the stair for.** She carries two loads at the fifth hour for the water butt, stops on the landing at one count, and tells a clerk's apprentice she wants the water carried. **Du Hong on the second stair: there is a number, and if she is ever calculating instead of looking he will say *walk* and she will say *entered*. She is eighteen; he has been eighteen since the autumn.** She asks the third desk where its sheets go: up at the quarter, same crate as the rest. No forgiveness asked or given. |
| 100 | What Being Chosen Costs | Tower loft alone: designation prices not shelters; eleven spaced because spent at different rates; honoured quests answer out of same foundation tax draws from, Chosen adds occasions. **She walks every occasion back through — trial, four seconds, the reading, the fever, the stage — and they were all occasions and not one was a cost somebody added for her.** He had not made her expensive; he had made her *often*, out of love with his mouth shut. She stops one step short of *who built this* and leaves it where it lay. **Then the last sum: she could stop taking — stay at two, be a servant with a god in her head, die with nothing entered against her — and she puts the version down, because she is not going to unask it at seventeen because the asking turned out to be expensive.** Tells him; three stand; does not forgive. Hears noise; goes down to tower foot where Du Hong is stopped. |
| 101 | The Listed Kill | Attempt at tower foot: Du Hong gives own name, burns one sheet, refuses place; beaten, left in thaw water. **The younger man reads his file aloud over a boy in the water — *advancement on the recommendation of Ning Yan, direction to report, never filed, two entries in one hand* — which is what puts him where she can reach him, and she enters that as a fifth cause at the burial.** She kills second man with Nine-Step habit, one arm, nearly killed; **the crack opens the whole way down while the killing is still in her legs, working without being asked, and for one breath she is aware of everyone in the tower above her.** Merit from listed kill only, told-before-spent, yes. **It does not feel like losing an arm: her legs are exactly as strong and she can no longer remember which of the two men she has to turn — the first move, not the shape.** Settlement ~11 weeks. One bracket, the listed kill settling. |
| 102 | Feng Holding the Lamp | He dies before night out with Feng holding lamp. **Feng says out loud that two ribs go in and he cannot put two back and he is the only hand in the tower — and makes her hear him say it before he does it, so she cannot later claim she did not.** She holds pressure, and there is no moment in the death she could have called the moment. **The Watch comes at the ninth hour, not at the killing, and writes a body, a road, a ditch, the shouting about a refused place and the shouting finished when it arrived, and a lamp carried up the stair. It cannot write that the corpse was answering to an entered act, because no office in Lowwater has a form with a column for it. That is why the eight hundred jin still stands.** Clerks write down a fall on the stair because it is easier than the other thing, and she lets them. Proof fails on body; bounty unclaimed. Settlement holds ~11 weeks. |
| 103 | Four Causes | Burial: four causes all hers — hand in file; copies in coat; surety; killing — **and a fifth she adds herself, that the second man would not have stood where she could reach him had he not been reading his file aloud over a boy in the water.** Signs the burial line, second document she has ever written naming her as the cause of anything. **At the county office she lodges it; a clerk asks whether she is a member of the household and she answers *I am the undersigned*; he stamps it with the same stamp as the pending filing. He confirms the surety and she asks him to write the transfer as *Stands to me*, and he writes it in the county book where a county keeps a line.** Surety void, debt hers; pending standing capped 5; claim standing; *kiln-born* in two heads; column five. Cheap door gone. |

### Batch 0009 (ch 104–118) — grief and the grant. VOLUME 2 CLOSED.

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 104 | The Ash-Flats | Ten days after the burial she trains grief on the Verge flats: one four-pound stone, thirty yards, all day, mud to the ankle. **He asks first and asks properly** — third term in the correct order, three days held, both choices and their costs set out, no recommendation. **She reads the filing's own words: it counts stages *as announced*, and a stage that is not announced goes nowhere.** She suppresses. **No bracketed line in the chapter.** Cost: the fourth stage reaches no county book, the chain of the filing takes a hole at the four, and she cannot know whether a registrar will call that an absence or a break. She buys a burial morning with it. Nine days of not eating begin. |
| 105 | Nine Days Unfed | Feng brings a bowl at the eighth hour, watches her get down four mouthfuls, and takes it away without a word — and the chapter says why on the page: a furnace crew does not ask twice about the pot, and a woman who is not eating is either lost the pot or worked somewhere she can be watched. The girl of twenty-four brings willow tea down to the trough three hours a day and says she will stop, because the eighth estate does not pay her to stand at a trough. **The third day is the worst and it is not the strength — the legs carry four hours and eight hundred pounds; what goes is the willingness to do a thing that is not on the schedule.** She signs the county hours with a hand that has a hole in it and refuses a reading back; a signature is a signature. **He states his limit once and without asking for anything: he cannot feed her, he has no hands.** On the ninth day she eats half a stranger's bowl at the trough and is sick and carries water for an hour. |
| 106 | Stands to Me | The county reads the transfer line out. **The clerk explains what a surety is actually for: not a name on a sheet but the right to be sick — *he was in that room so that a day could be a day.*** With him dead there is nothing else in the room and a missed hour is a gap and a gap breaks a filing. **She asks for it in writing — no surety again without relicensing — and the clerk stamps it, and does not ask why.** She walks the road both ways alone and enters the walking. The dead man had bought her a hundred days of being ill. She had spent them being at the hour, so they are the same thing. Four months of travel has already cost him eleven weeks and about six are left. |
| 107 | The Quiet Lane | Two men on a wall in the lane, four days, buying nothing, saying good morning to a barrow. **Pursuit has not ended; it has become attendance.** A thing paid for by the day does not need to be hidden to be paid for; the buyer is not in Lowwater and is not in a hurry. The claim does not decay and does not need the cheap door again — eight of nine are left and every one is dearer. **She offers them four coins and the nearer one says no: *we are not to take anything off anybody in this lane, we are to be here.*** Ninety seconds to learn exactly what an unanswered claim is made of: a standing instruction with a body attached when somebody decides the body is cheap enough. Four months of doors shut behind her and not one of them opened; now there is no door at all. |
| 108 | Stone and Paper | Three days on the intake plank: two hundred and forty hands a day, five columns, the sixth column for the ninth hour, the clerk's apprentice of fifteen who is told not to thank her by Feng's standing order. **She learns what a clerk's page looks like from inside: entirely truthful, and no line on any form says which.** A bundle in a new hooked brown-edged hand goes up the quarter in the crate and she reads only the edge, because turning is taking. **The good coat comes down the lane, reads the board, asks for the fifth, is told the sixth, puts a hand in his coat and leaves** — he came for a body and a lane has a column in it with fifty-one names. Two offices, four days apart: one wants her in the room, the other wants a number out of the mountain. The schedule must be certified at the Hall's clerk. She is going up. |
| 109 | The Safest Thing Here | The girl of twenty-four, alive, leg crooked, four paid hours out of six, copying carriers' lists for the eighth estate and the carrier's sheets besides. **She gives the thing that matters and nothing else: the county has asked in writing, on the ordinary form, for the current season's furnace schedule with its hours certified, for attachment to a filing already standing — it has been sitting on a shelf in the third room ever since, because nobody knows what a furnace schedule is and nobody wanted to send it back wrong.** She enters it on the return sheet **on her own hour**, which is the one thing she controls. Ning Yan almost thanks her and does not, because the girl has set the not-being-thanked up in advance as protection, and saying it would take an hour off a morning she is paid four for. Eight days of road. A furnace floor she will never own. |
| 110 | The Sealed Slip | A sealed slip up in the quarter's crate on ordinary county stationery, nothing above the county mark. **A request from outside the bound that the term standing in the second position of the status column be struck wherever it is copied — naming the term by its position and not by its characters**, which is why a keeper can act on it without reading it. **Feng says the order of his own three sentences: I cannot read it; I am going to copy the shape of it; when they come I can lay two sheets down and tell the truth both ways.** He copies the shape for an hour on a stool she cannot watch from, and strikes the four walled county copies, his working sheet and the shelf copy, dry brush, no smudge. **She does not ask him to recognise the hand and he recognises nothing.** The hand is an ordinary Lowwater county hand and gives Yuan nothing at all. **The office attached has a name he cannot read — fourth time he has said so, and this time in front of a witness.** Nothing answers what eleven became. Two heads hold the word; the paper goes. Column stays at five. |
| 111 | Struck Wherever Copied | The county strikes the term from the second position of her own advancement sheet at the second hour, with the hour entered, while she stands in the ante-room, **not asked to leave and not asked to stay and not consulted.** She does not ask to see the original. The sheet now reads exactly like any other county's sheet and nothing in it is false. **She learns what the countersignature actually needs: the labour, the season's schedule, certified by the office that keeps it, returned at the quarter — and the county does not keep furnace schedules.** She goes up and looks at the four copies: a line through one cell reads as a clerk tidying a sheet. **The volumes on the high shelf are not copies and are not struck, and nobody is going to climb four flights to find out. The door closes on a shelf, not a silence.** One line of plain text in the docket, no code, and she never writes the word down again. Feng gives her a cart pass and will not take it back. |
| 112 | Nine Men | Fourth bridge: `Ning Yan. Held.` gone over in the spring by the same hand. Old Gao told her at the trestle that the tower foot was known on the lane before the snow left the Verge. The foreman puts her on the cold end in front of the shift. **Nine men do not say nothing: two of them say *she did it* and *she buried him herself* at the bottom of the stair, to each other and not to her.** She offers to write it in the book the way she wrote the other two; the foreman says *nobody is going to sign for you.* Six days, and the rule about marks goes unused because no man in that crew wants his name on a line of hers. **Shen Xiu reads the sheet twice, never looks at her, enters 311 and not the intention, and one beat at the end where his eye stays half a count longer on the signature than on the numbers — and he says nothing.** She pays eleven days in the last week of the thaw and the book closes square. Then the Hall, with no roof, and rain going through it four feet from the clerk's head. |
| 113 | The Roofless Hall | She puts the filing copy, the certified schedule and her defect line on the Hall table and asks for it to go up **ahead of a crate**, in plain words, with eight days of road in the open. **What the sponsors do is the chapter: Lu Kang reads the whole schedule, does not look at the nine households, takes the Hall's stamp off the wall and stamps it himself, and says nothing at all.** She says it to his face that the Foundation writ died on the county sheet the hour she signed the ceiling, and Pei An's answer is that it has not been revoked and Lu Kang's is that it has no road left to anywhere. **She does not thank him: he stamped his own paper, which was the only lawful thing left in the building.** The leash, the lever and the room, in one stamp, in the rain. Then the cold end at the ninth hour, and a crew that has worked out where she works and moved to the other end of the room. |
| 114 | Glad of the Noise | Fourth hour of the afternoon, the cold end, nine men two hundred yards off. The fifth comes up through the fourth and goes round it, the crack opens the whole way down, and for one breath she can feel the paper on the top of the fifth stage the way a man feels a roof. **She orders it released while she is standing up, on the terrace-and-step argument.** The layer speaks once: *Assembly Hall of Qinghe. Ning Yan of the Qinghe furnace crew, servant-born, entered on the furnace schedule of this Hall. Nothing further is let go. Condensing, stage five, at Qinghe is held.* **The one bracketed line in the batch, and the wording carries the ceiling.** **The noise is the record** — the first time she is glad of it, and glad because a suppressed stage is a hole and an announced one is a line in a county book. Cost stated plain by him and accepted: nine counties, a tower, a county clerk with her mark on two documents. Nine men stop working and look at her. A man carries her channel bar up the stair because she cannot lift it and she did not ask. |
| 115 | Countersignature | Fourth in the queue in the wet. Sun He reads the schedule **twice**, because it is the thing the hours were done in and not a list of hours. **The gap: the sheet reads one, two, three and then five.** She states it flat — *because I did not want nine counties to be told what I had become on the morning they buried a man I killed* — and the room waits. **Sun He's ruling, read aloud: an absence is not a breach.** He writes the distinction onto the sheet so the next man does not have to make it himself, and says he cannot restore the fourth stage and would not have if she had asked. Then the stamp. **Scope: draw sect qi, take paid work outside the crew, be taught past her stage, under countersignature at this county.** **Six conditions read to the queue, the sixth being that the eight hundred jin standing is entered on the licence, travels with it, and is not affected by it.** He could have refused again and says what actually changed: the labour is on the sheet, and it is worth exactly a stamp. Then he looks at her for the first time and says *you will be careful on a road.* **Feng stood four feet behind her the whole four hours, read nothing, wrote one line in fair hand with nothing in it that could be carried upward, and said it was like copying.** |
| 116 | The Conditions | She reads the licence in the open on the benches by the well, because a secret on a first day is something somebody finds on the second. Four of six are ordinary. The first is *no surety may stand for her again without relicensing*, and she understands what it will be at the end of it: a woman who is in a room every hour for fifteen years does not die in a bed. **The sixth is on the back in the county's own hand and it is eight hundred jin.** The good coat is on the square in the rain with his tube and no coin, and he has just been handed by an office doing its duty the instrument of his trade — **she has bought her own advancement with a document naming her to the one man paid to be able to name her.** He puts a coin on a shut desk, picks it up, and goes home, which is the first thing she has seen him do that is not part of a trade. The count: four weeks off the last day of the thaw to the crate, a working count of five weeks, and Sun He reads a return in the first week after it, so the return is the fifth week of summer; the road shuts; a licence does not carry a writ for the pass. **She has a room, and it is the size of the paper.** |
| 117 | Nailed Above the Second Bunk | Nailed crooked over the foot of the second bunk with one arm, because that is the only place she has ever lived in that was hers. **The crew reads it aloud: *Condensing five. It says five. We were not done with three.*** She tells them plainly that three of the four they were not done with was hers to announce and was not, and that paper says what she is and not what she is worth, and she is not asking anybody to sign. Then the whole of the volume, item by item and without softening: the plate, the four registers reading *reconciled* for a living brother on a list beyond the Wall, Du Hong and two friends in the ground, the ninth man in the doorway who signed the sheet that killed two of his own crew, three sponsors holding a dead writ in silence, Shen Xiu who will never be thanked, Hou Sanliang owed by nobody and owed nothing, the girl of twenty-four alive and the safest thing in that town, two heads holding a word that is on no paper in nine counties. **Feng, at the back of a fair volume off the warm shelf: one line, fair hand, entered upward with the quarter, entered here, not entered in the column.** Column five. **She asks him to go quiet, which is the first time all year she has asked him for nothing.** |
| 118 | A Second Cough | Rain in four places onto the furnace floor schedule; two hands of it lifted edge-first; the ink of nine hands running downhill into an hour column. The Hall clerk says the name is the wrong order and has no other version, and writes it in the wrong order. **The licence nailed over the bunk with a line of rain down the second condition, and the defect line going soft at the crease, and about a month from a hole in the middle of *permanent*.** **How much is left: a little under three-quarters, about six weeks.** The arithmetic against the calendar: five weeks to the quarter's return, the first return in the fifth week of summer and it has to be in the county book in the fourth, a road that shuts — six weeks is not enough for a licence with five weeks in it before its first return. **She does not tell him to stop. She gives four conditions — hours only, no Merit off her, nothing decided about her body including the rate, no stage above five, and he tells her on the day it is gone. He gives three, unchanged since the ninth step.** Then Feng's sheet comes down with the crate: a second cough in the stairwell that is not his, a man at the first desk twice a week who buys nothing, tells carriers he is not from the Bureau, and gives his name to nobody. **Unnamed. The lamp burning down the mountain.** |

## Volume 2 closed — audited

**Audited against `outline/volume-02.md` line by line at the volume close. PASS on every named item
except fifteen word-level repairs, all now made in the chapters.** No title, chapter order, card beat,
bracket or ending moved. The audit table and the repair note are in `state/current.md` under
`## Volume 2 close audit`. **What the close changed on the page, for the next batch to read first:**

- **The crew quarters hold nine men and her — ten sleepers, twelve bunks.** Five places in 113–118
  had drifted to *ten men*, making eleven occupants, and ch 118 had enumerated eleven outright.
  Repaired throughout.
- **The claim is the sixth condition and the last thing on the back of the licence.** 115 and 116
  called it the fourth from the bottom three times. Repaired.
- **The eleven entries run six hundred years.** 111 said *four hundred* twice against six hundred
  stated four times since ch 57. Repaired. **Ch 96's *the last entry was four hundred years back* and
  ch 110's *a thing four hundred years old* are a different figure — the gap since the previous
  designation — and both stand.**
- **Two heads hold *kiln-born*: hers and Yuan's.** 111's log line had made it three. Feng holds a
  struck shape and no word. Repaired.
- **The county struck the second position of her advancement sheet days before 113 and 115.** Both
  said *this morning*; she was four miles up a mountain and then four miles back down. Rewritten.
- **The tower is four miles from the quarters**, as it has been since ch 59. Ch 118's *eleven hundred
  yards* repaired.
- **Volume 2 ran from the sixth of December to the last day of the thaw** — about five and a half
  months, not the four the plan called. The page reconciles with itself: ch 115's *eleven weeks*
  against ch 116's *six weeks ago*, ch 114's *six weeks to the return* against ch 118's *five weeks*.


**Ch 104–118 (Volume 2, batch 0009).** Grief and the grant. She buries the fourth stage on a mud flat so that nine counties are not told what she became on the morning they buried Du Hong, and the stage reaches no county book in nine counties and never will. Nine days of not eating that Yuan cannot fix and cannot even name the shape of. She works the filing with no surety behind her and learns that the dead man had been buying her the right to be sick. The pursuit stops and becomes two men on a wall who buy nothing, because an unanswered claim does not decay and a patient creditor has lost nothing by waiting. **A sealed slip on ordinary county stationery carries a request from outside the bound to strike the term in the second position of the status column wherever it is copied — naming it by position, not by characters — and Feng copies the shape of it without reading it, strikes his copies, and keeps the shape; the office has a name Yuan cannot read for the fourth time, and nothing answers what became of the eleven.** She goes up the mountain for the schedule the countersignature needs and comes home to a crew of nine who will not sign her shift and who say *she did it* at the bottom of the stair, not to her. Lu Kang certifies the schedule in silence because it is the only thing on it that ever worked. She takes Condensing five at her own order on the cold end and lets it go, and the sentence says *nothing further is let go*, and it is the first time she has been glad of the noise. **Sun He finds the gap in her record, rules in the open that an absence is not a breach, and countersigns the Licence to Practise Past Registration to Condensing stage 5 with the furnace schedule attached as collateral labour and six conditions on the back — the sixth of which enters the eight hundred jin standing claim on the licence itself.** She walks out legal, capped, grieving, and priced. She nails it crooked over her own bunk. Settlement a little under three-quarters, about six weeks. She does not forgive him. **A stranger is at the first desk in Lowwater twice a week, buying nothing, telling carriers he is not from the Bureau, and giving his name to nobody.** Power: **Condensing 5, and no further — she signed the ceiling herself.** Volume closed.

## Volume 3 — The Hunter (ch 119–178)

### Batch 0001 (ch 119–132) — examination and exposure. WRITTEN, 36,809 words over fourteen chapters.

| Ch | Title | Summary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| 119 | The Hire Sheet | Lowwater, day 2. The lane behind the well, four tables, one man with a board. The hirer's sheet is not a county sheet: nobody certifies it, nobody copies it upward, nobody has ever heard of it. She signs in her left hand because the right one is out about one time in three, and the man reads it correctly in one go and does not ask why. Six loads to a water channel at twelve coppers. **She has never been paid by somebody who did not already own her**, and she wants it. Feng will enter the ninth hour as a tower change, not a request. Log: a board with chalk on it and eleven strangers who know nothing. |
| 120 | Where the Work Is | Days 4–5. A foreman calls her *the licensed one* at the oil end in front of eleven people: a description, cheaper than a name, travels further, no office can strike it. The mat comes off her shoulder in public at the third hour; she gets it back in four minutes and the man at the board says her deduction out loud so everyone hears it came off the licensed one and nobody else. Eleven strangers working beside her every day have not asked her one question, and that is the cure she has never been given. **A carrier brings the word: the man at the first desk has stopped asking what a schedule is and started asking what hours a licensed woman works, and wrote licensed woman on the back of his hand.** She keeps the lane. Irreversible. |
| 121 | The Nineteen Minutes | Day 8. The county hour is at the third hour and the lane is nineteen minutes from the square, so every county hour costs four loads — half a day's pay, permanently. No surety may stand for her, so there is nobody to send. **He tells her the arm is going before it goes**, and it is Voice, near her, costing nothing, and telling her is not deciding; she finds out that her whole project of testing twice a day has been removed and there is nothing she can do with it. The entry in the county book is true: attended, the hour entered, the margin nobody reads. The licence survives its own first test and she says so out loud into a room. Non-forgiveness stated once, briefly, and not argued. |
| 122 | The First Desk | Day 11, market. He asks her one question in front of a queue and it is the wrong question for a bounty man and the right question for a clerk: **who certifies your hours.** She has four moves available including saying nothing at all and walking on, and she uses none of them. She answers truthfully, in daylight, that the Hall's clerk at Qinghe certifies them under a maintaining member's stamp and they return at the quarter — six words she has never said out loud in a market, and cannot un-tell.
